# Simulation report

Runs: 4800. Case and input pairs: 800. Configurations: 6. Seeds: 1, 2, 3, 4, 5.

Unguarded runs: 0 of 4800. Unguarded runs have no protection against long waits beyond the inputs being finite.

Configurations appear in a fixed order. This report makes no judgement between them: a person reads the tables and charts and decides.

Each cell shows the mean across seeds, then the range across seeds in brackets. That range describes spread and isn't a significance test: configurations run the same inputs, so compare them seed by seed.

Total time is wait time plus a fixed distance, so differences in mean total time are exactly the differences in mean wait time.

Sections marked fixed load give a case the same arrivals as the building it is compared with, whose own sections are that load. Cases of another height keep the arrival ticks, with floors drawn for their height.
